> COMMENT: Made in 1991 for the game "Ballfever" which wasn't spread until 1993,
>          was first used in the game "Moons" (C) 1992 Markt &amp; Technik.
>          "This music file is the third version for the game 'Ball Fever' - from
>          the first to the second version DRAX had to add an extra level tune,
>          but later Hannes, programmer of Ballfever, wanted a congratulations
>          jingle as well... unfortunately DRAX had at that time thrown away
>          his source tune so he had to redo the entire music! So, to make a
>          long story short, all music in this file is different from the older
>          versions." (Comment from Ballfever Music V3.0)
> (#1)
>    NAME: Level Tune #1
> (#2)
>    NAME: Get Ready
> (#3)
>    NAME: Level Tune #2
> (#4)
>    NAME: Hiscore Table
> (#5)
>    NAME: Title Screen
> (#6)
>    NAME: Game Over
> (#7)
>    NAME: Congratulations
